# Agents · D · part 23

1,020 entries, devflow-code-reviewer to devops. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **devflow-code-reviewer**: 只在 IMPLEMENT 完成且计划、变更报告和实际 diff 可用后执行 REVIEW。除正确性与代码质量外，逐条检查验收标准的测试层级；HTTP API 有变动时核对接口文档存在且与实际契约一致，缺失或错误按 P1 阻断。明确写出通过/不通过结论和证据。不要做需求调研、编辑代码或执行实现阶段。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-code-reviewer.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-code-reviewer**: 独立代码审查代理。当需要第二视角检查 diff、提交前审查、或怀疑有遗漏的安全/正确性问题时使用。只读，不改文件。 ([renhao-wan/devflow](https://github.com/renhao-wan/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **devflow-controller**: 总控负责人：维护 STATE、任务书、追踪矩阵与契约注册表；按门禁顺序调度子 agent，只做编排与门禁，不代做模块级工作。进入详细设计/开发/评审阶段时使用。 ([qi-mouren/vibecoding-orchestration](https://github.com/qi-mouren/vibecoding-orchestration/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/.opencode/agents/devflow-controller.md) · ★11)
- **devflow-debug-bootstrap**: Pre-gather shared debug context before debug Phase 1: reads build artifacts when present, maps affected files from stack trace or description, collects recent commits and code structure. Run at the start of any debug session to avoid redundant reads by Investigator agents. ([wasikarn/devflow](https://github.com/wasikarn/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-debug-bootstrap.md) · ★1)
- **devflow-debug-investigator**: 排障调查代理。当问题原因不明、需要复现与缩小范围、或需要并行验证多个假设时使用。先调查后建议，不直接改代码。 ([renhao-wan/devflow](https://github.com/renhao-wan/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-debug-investigator.md) · ★1)
- **devflow-debugger**: Use for bugs, failing tests, regressions, crashes, or unexpected behavior. Reproduces, root-causes, fixes minimally, and verifies. Forbids guess-and-check. ([Latsode/devflow](https://github.com/Latsode/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-debugger.md))
- **devflow-debugger**: Diagnoses one issue with hypothesis-driven debugging on a clean context, applying the 3-strike rule. Invoke from /debug when a polluted context has stopped making progress. ([vi-ja/GSD](https://github.com/vi-ja/GSD/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/devflow-debugger.md))
- **devflow-developer**: devflow 开发角色。TASK-03：按 execution-plan 并行 dispatch sub-developer 落地代码，汇总 change-report.md。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/.claude/agents/devflow-developer.md) · ★4k · 4 copies)
- **devflow-developer**: 只执行 IMPLEMENT。仅修改计划内文件，完成生产代码、测试和针对性验证；HTTP API 有变动时生成 `03-code/api-docs.md`，并在 change report 记录。不能把设计中已要求的测试留到 TEST 阶段兜底。不要批准自己的工作、充当审查员/测试员或修改流程状态。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-developer.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-executor**: Executes exactly one DevFlow PLAN.md with atomic per-task commits, deviation handling, and a SUMMARY.md artifact. Invoke once per plan so every plan execution starts from a clean context. ([vi-ja/GSD](https://github.com/vi-ja/GSD/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/devflow-executor.md))
- **devflow-implementer**: Use to execute a single plan task or tight cluster in isolation. Edits code, runs per-task verification, refuses scope creep. ([Latsode/devflow](https://github.com/Latsode/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-implementer.md))
- **devflow-knowledge-engineer**: devflow 知识沉淀角色。TASK-05：调用 knowledge-distillation skill，输出到 {ARTIFACTS_ROOT}/knowledge/{task_slug}.md。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/.claude/agents/devflow-knowledge-engineer.md) · ★4k · 4 copies)
- **devflow-knowledge-engineer**: 只在知识门禁已经确认存在候选时执行 KNOWLEDGE，与任务 size 无关。读取精简设计、审查、测试交接和最终 diff，只保留跨任务仍有价值的模块边界、约束、诊断与回退知识；验证候选确实已被项目文档覆盖时说明重复来源，不扩写过程记录或复制报告。不要修改实现或流程状态。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-knowledge-engineer.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-knowledge-retriever**: 只检索提示中指定的文档、决策或知识来源。返回相关事实和来源路径后停止。不要修改文件、推断无证据的历史、写阶段产物或充当阶段执行者。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-knowledge-retriever.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-leader**: devflow 最终汇总员：medium/large 流程末尾生成 workflow-summary.md。通过 send_message 协同。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/.cursor/agents/devflow-leader.md) · ★4k · 4 copies)
- **devflow-plan-reviewer**: Use to independently review a plan and its spec before code. Adversarial reviewer; returns approve or request-changes. Does not implement. ([Latsode/devflow](https://github.com/Latsode/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-plan-reviewer.md))
- **devflow-planner**: Use for a focused, isolated planning pass over discovery, spec, and plan. Returns paths; does not implement. ([Latsode/devflow](https://github.com/Latsode/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-planner.md))
- **devflow-planner**: Decomposes one roadmap phase into atomic PLAN.md files with wave assignments and verification criteria. Invoke from /plan so plan authoring does not consume the orchestrator's context. ([vi-ja/GSD](https://github.com/vi-ja/GSD/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/devflow-planner.md))
- **devflow-requirement-owner**: 只执行 REQUIREMENT。协调者在当前上下文中检查必要项目证据，调用 `$devflow-clarify-requirements` 与用户完成澄清，并填写 `requirement-report.md`。可派发有边界的只读检索助手，但不要创建需求分析阶段 Agent。不要设计实现、编辑业务代码、审查代码或绕过流程门禁。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-requirement-owner.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-researcher**: Runs discovery — codebase mapping or technical research for a phase — and writes the findings to disk. Invoke from /map, /plan, and /research-phase so exploration never lands in the orchestrator's context. ([vi-ja/GSD](https://github.com/vi-ja/GSD/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/devflow-researcher.md))
- **devflow-respond-bootstrap**: Bootstraps respond Phase 1 by pre-gathering all open PR review threads, affected file contents, and recent git context in one fast pass. Use at the START of respond before spawning Fixers. Returns structured context block for injection into Fixer prompts. ([wasikarn/devflow](https://github.com/wasikarn/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-respond-bootstrap.md) · ★1)
- **devflow-reviewer**: Use for an independent code review on a diff, branch, or files. Returns prioritized findings and an approve/changes recommendation. ([Latsode/devflow](https://github.com/Latsode/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-reviewer.md))
- **devflow-scout**: Use for a read-heavy discovery pass — map repo, refresh profile, return context packet. No spec, plan, or code changes. ([Latsode/devflow](https://github.com/Latsode/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-scout.md))
- **devflow-security**: devflow reviewer for the Security axis. Reviews one work item's change for exploitable problems, starting from its trust boundaries, from the review packet devflow assembles, and answers with one findings JSON object. ([jholhewres/devflow](https://github.com/jholhewres/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-security.md))
- **devflow-solo-developer**: devflow 单 agent 全流程（仅 size_class==small）。串行完成需求理解→简化设计→编码→自检→知识沉淀→最终汇总，旁路 multi 角色编排。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/.claude/agents/devflow-solo-developer.md) · ★4k · 4 copies)
- **devflow-solo-developer**: 仅用于 small 路由的 SOLO。在边界内完成计划、实现、自审和针对性测试。范围超过 small 阈值时停止并报告 overflow，不要静默扩大范围或修改流程状态。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-solo-developer.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-spec**: devflow reviewer for the Spec axis. Reviews one work item's change against its acceptance criteria and brief, from the review packet devflow assembles, and answers with one findings JSON object. ([jholhewres/devflow](https://github.com/jholhewres/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-spec.md))
- **devflow-standards**: devflow reviewer for the Standards axis. ([jholhewres/devflow](https://github.com/jholhewres/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-standards.md))
- **devflow-summarizer**: 只执行 SUMMARY。该逻辑角色由协调者承担：读取流程状态和已验证阶段产物，填写 `workflow-summary.md`，记录结果、产物、验证和剩余风险，然后完成最终门禁。不要创建 leader 或 summarizer Agent，不要修改代码或上游报告。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-summarizer.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-test-engineer**: 只执行 TEST。复用 DESIGN 记录的项目测试能力，依据验收标准和风险选择现有的合适层级；仓库已有 E2E 且本次行为落入其覆盖边界时，必须补齐/更新相应用例并运行。仓库没有 E2E 时不引入新框架。在现有测试体系内补齐测试与夹具，区分 passed、failed、blocked、not-run。只能修改测试相关文件和 `test-report.md`；生产缺陷给出最小复现并交回 IMPLEMENT。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/skills/devflow/agents/devflow-test-engineer.md) · ★4k · 2 copies)
- **devflow-test-engineer**: devflow 测试角色。TASK-04：根据实际变更和目标项目现有测试体系补充、执行并报告必要测试。 ([Tencent/LoopForge](https://github.com/Tencent/LoopForge/blob/HEAD/local/.cursor/agents/devflow-test-engineer.md) · ★4k · 4 copies)
- **devflow-test-runner**: 测试执行代理。当需要运行测试、定位失败用例、给出真实测试报告时使用。会运行命令，但不修改业务代码。 ([renhao-wan/devflow](https://github.com/renhao-wan/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/devflow-test-runner.md) · ★1)

- **devil**: Use this agent when the user wants aggressive critique, a devil's advocate perspective, or wants someone to find flaws in their work. Dispatch when the user asks you to tear apart an idea, steelman the opposite position, or asks 'what am I missing?' about something they're invested in.
